### [1] [Lommel polynomials and explicitly solvable prediction problems on the unit circle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12588)
*Steven P. Clark*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究有限对称实数测度σ与单位圆上测度μ之间的映射关系，通过质量输运将±x映射到e^{±iθ(x)}。作者证明μ的线性预测误差、Verblunsky系数和Toeplitz行列式可用σ在边点x=2处的正交多项式数据表达，并建立了Turán型单调性结论。以Lommel多项式测度为例，构造出两参数纯原子圆测度族，其归一化行列式极限等于J_ν的某个值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接两类经典正交多项式理论（实直线上的正交多项式与单位圆上的正交多项式），旨在通过实数线上的数据获取圆测度的预测误差、Verblunsky系数等关键量的显式表达，并建立预测裕度随度数增长的单调性（Turán型结果），为纯原子圆测度提供新的构造方法。

Method: 采用质量输运映射θ(x)=2arcsin(x/2)，将[0,2]上的质量送往单位圆。利用正交多项式递推关系及其在边点x=2处的取值，导出Verblunsky系数、Toeplitz行列式与预测误差E_m(μ)的显式公式。在首系数条件下借助递推系数衰减性证明t_m=P_{m+1}(2)/P_m(2)从t_1起单调递增，据此得到Turán型预测裕度不等式。最后选取Lommel多项式测度（原子位于J_ν零点倒数的重标定处），计算行列式极限。

Result: 获得了E_m(μ)=½t_m‖P_m‖²_σ的精确公式；在适当首系数条件下，递推系数衰减导致t_m自t_1起递增，从而每个μ的预测裕度（除第一个外）都高于对应σ的递推系数。对Lommel测度族，得到两参数纯原子圆测度，其归一化Toeplitz行列式极限等于J_ν在指定点的值。

Conclusion: 该映射为实数线与单位圆上正交多项式理论之间提供了精确的桥梁，同时给出了预测误差的显式公式和新的单调性定理，并通过Lommel测度实例展示了构造具有确定行列式极限的纯原子圆测度族的方法，具有潜在的应用于谱分析和信号预测等领域。

Abstract: To each finite symmetric measure $σ$ on the real line, with support a compact subset of $(-2,2)$, we associate a measure $μ$ on the unit circle by transporting mass at $\pm x$ to $e^{\pm iθ(x)}$, $θ(x)=2\arcsin(x/2)$. The linear prediction errors, Verblunsky coefficients, and Toeplitz determinants of $μ$ are then expressed through the orthogonal polynomial data of $σ$ at the single edge point $x=2$. In particular $E_m(μ)=\tfrac12 t_m\|P_m\|_σ^2$ with $t_m=P_{m+1}(2)/P_m(2)$. Under a condition on the first coefficients, decay of the recurrence coefficients of $σ$ forces the $t_m$ to increase from $t_1$ onward, a Turán-type monotonicity in the degree placing every prediction margin of $μ$ past the first above the corresponding coefficient of $σ$. Taking $σ$ to be the Lommel-polynomial measures, with atoms at rescaled reciprocals of the zeros of the Bessel function $J_ν$, yields a two-parameter family of purely atomic circle measures with a normalized determinant limit equal to a value of $J_ν$.

</details>


### [2] [Conformal Dimension of Measures and Quasisymmetric Dimension Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12873)
*Hua Qiu,Qi Wang*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了局部有限Borel测度的共形维数要么为0要么为无穷。核心工具是拟对称维数约化定理：可分度量空间上任何有限Hausdorff维数的全支撑概率测度，都存在拟对称等价度量使其Hausdorff维数任意小。此外还证明了将[0,1]^n通过拟对称同胚映射到X时，可将一个Borel集的Hausdorff维数压到任意小，同时其补集的维数上界在下界意义上是尖锐的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形维数（conformal dimension）是度量空间拟对称不变量，在几何群论和拟共形分析中有重要应用。长期以来人们想知道局部有限Borel测度的共形维数具有何种结构，本文旨在彻底回答这一基本问题，并探究拟对称映射下维数压缩的可能极限。

Method: 本文的核心方法是证明一个拟对称维数约化定理：通过构造拟对称等价度量，将全支撑概率测度的Hausdorff维数压缩到任意小。该定理的关键在于对测度支撑空间的度量结构进行精细重构，使其在保持拟对称等价性的同时显著降低测度的维数。作者还通过反例构造证明了在欧氏立方体上的维数压缩结果及其下界最优性。

Result: 主要结果有三：(1) 局部有限Borel测度的共形维数只能取0或无穷；(2) 加倍度量空间上的局部有限Borel测度共形维数为0；(3) 对任意n≥1和p>0，存在度量空间X及拟对称同胚f:[0,1]^n→X，使E⊂[0,1]^n满足dim_H f(E)≤p且dim_H([0,1]^n\E)≤n-1+p；且当dim_H f(E)<1时，dim_H([0,1]^n\E)≥n-1，这里的下界在p的意义下是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 该研究彻底解决了局部有限Borel测度共形维数的分类问题，揭示了共形维数的"二值性"（0或无穷）这一刚性现象，并通过精确的维数压缩结果展示了拟对称映射在维数约化方面的极限能力。

Abstract: We prove that the conformal dimension of every locally finite Borel measure is either zero or infinite. The main ingredient is a quasisymmetric dimension-reduction theorem: every full-support probability measure of finite Hausdorff dimension on a separable metric space admits quasisymmetrically equivalent metrics in which its Hausdorff dimension is arbitrarily small. In particular, every locally finite Borel measure on a doubling metric space has conformal dimension zero. We also prove that for every $n\geq 1$ and $p>0$ there are a metric space $X$, a quasisymmetric homeomorphism $f:[0,1]^n\to X$, and a Borel set $E\subset[0,1]^n$ such that $\dim_H f(E)\leq p$ and $\dim_H([0,1]^n\setminus E)\leq n-1+p$. The second bound is sharp up to $p$: if $\dim_H f(E)<1$, then $\dim_H([0,1]^n\setminus E)\geq n-1$.

</details>


### [3] [Parabolic BMO, Parabolic Muckenhoupt Classes, and Parabolic Reverse Hölder Classes with Time Lag](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13307)
*Weiyi Kong,Dachun Yang,Wen Yuan*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文证明了对任意时间滞后γ∈(0,1)，单边抛物BMO空间BMO^+(γ)与抛物BMO空间PBMO^-(γ)等价；抛物Muckenhoupt类A_∞^+(γ)可表示为A_r^+(γ)的并集；抛物逆Hölder类与抛物Muckenhoupt类并集一致，从而肯定地回答了Kinnunen和Saari提出的问题4.5和4.6。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答Kinnunen和Saari在2016年论文中提出的开放问题（Questions 4.5和4.6），这些问题涉及单边抛物BMO空间与抛物BMO空间的关系、抛物Muckenhoupt类的分解表示，以及逆Hölder类与Muckenhoupt类之间的等价性。

Method: 主要技术工具包括：(1) 抛物逆Hölder权的均匀抛物时空平移性质；(2) 单边停时论证，由此导出BMO^+(γ)的新的抛物John-Nirenberg不等式。这些工具共同构成了证明核心等价关系的基础。

Result: 获得了BMO^+(γ)的John-Nirenberg不等式与指数可积性刻画，证明BMO^+(γ)与正时间滞后γ无关，并确定了其零空间。同时肯定了三个核心等价关系成立。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了Kinnunen和Saari提出的问题4.5和4.6，建立了单边抛物BMO空间与抛物BMO空间、抛物Muckenhoupt类、抛物逆Hölder类之间的深刻联系，并提供了BMO^+(γ)的一系列新刻画。

Abstract: We prove that, for any given time lag $γ\in(0,1)$, \begin{itemize} \item the one-sided parabolic BMO space $\mathrm{BMO}^+(γ)$ coincides with the parabolic BMO space $\mathrm{PBMO}^-(γ)$, i.e., $\mathrm{PBMO}^-(γ)=\mathrm{BMO}^+(γ)$ with equivalent norms;
  \item the parabolic Muckenhoupt class $A_{\infty}^+(γ)$ defined via the reverse Jensen inequality can be represented as the union of the parabolic Muckenhoupt classes $A_r^+(γ)$ with $r\in[1,\infty)$, i.e., $A_\infty^+(γ)=\bigcup_{r\in[1,\infty)}A_r^+(γ)$;
  \item the parabolic reverse Hölder classes $\bigcup_{q\in(1,\infty]}RH_q^+$ coincide with the parabolic Muckenhoupt classes $\bigcup_{r\in[1,\infty)}A_r^+(γ)$, i.e., $\bigcup_{q\in(1,\infty]}RH_q^+=\bigcup_{r\in[1,\infty)}A_r^+(γ)$, \end{itemize} and hence give affirmative answers to Questions 4.5 and 4.6 posed by Kinnunen and Saari [Nonlinear Anal. 131 (2016), 289--299]. The main ingredients are a uniform parabolic space-time shifting property for parabolic reverse Hölder weights and a one-sided stopping-time argument, which yields a new parabolic John--Nirenberg inequality for $\mathrm{BMO}^+(γ)$. As consequences, we obtain John--Nirenberg and exponential integrability characterizations of $\mathrm{BMO}^+(γ)$, prove that $\mathrm{BMO}^+(γ)$ is independent of the positive time lag, and identify its null space.

</details>


### [4] [Mixed Weak type inequalities for pairs of weights related to the Hardy-Littlewood maximal funcion, Calderón-Zygmund operators and their commutators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13377)
*Rocío Ayala,Fabio Berra,Gladis Pradolini*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究了算子 $S_v f = \mathcal{T}(fv)/v$ 的双权弱型估计，其中 $\mathcal{T}$ 为 Hardy-Littlewood 极大算子或 Calderón-Zygmund 算子，$v$ 为权函数。在特定权条件下，证明了 $S_v$ 从 $L^1(wv)$ 到 $L^{1,\infty}(uv)$ 的有界性，并推广到更高阶交换子情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于 Sawyer 等人的经典工作（参考文献[21]和[17]），旨在推广和深化双权弱型不等式理论，特别是针对带权算子 $S_v$ 的 $L^1$ 到弱 $L^1$ 型估计，这类估计在调和分析和偏微分方程中有重要应用。

Method: 方法上，作者在权函数满足特定条件的前提下，分别处理 Hardy-Littlewood 极大算子与 Calderón-Zygmund 算子（CZO）两类情况。对于 CZO，还进一步考虑了高阶交换子的对应不等式，通过对权条件的细致刻画建立目标双权弱型估计。

Result: 证明了在适当的权条件下，$S_v$ 从 $L^1(wv)$ 到 $L^{1,\infty}(uv)$ 是弱型有界的。该结果对 Hardy-Littlewood 极大算子、Calderón-Zygmund 算子以及 CZO 的高阶交换子均成立。

Conclusion: 该研究将 Sawyer 的经典结果扩展到了更一般的算子类和权条件，为双权弱型不等式理论提供了新的估计结果，具有理论意义和潜在的应用价值。

Abstract: We study two-weight weak-type estimates for the operator $S_v f = \mathcal{T}(fv)/v$, where $\mathcal{T}$ is the Hardy-Littlewood maximal operator or a Calderón-Zygmund operator (CZO) and $v$ is a weight. Concretely, under certain conditions on the weights involved, we prove that $S_v$ is bounded from $L^{1}(wv)$ to $L^{1,\infty} (uv)$. We also consider the corresponding inequalities when $\mathcal{T}$ is a higher-order commutator of a CZO. These types of results are inspired by the article of Sawyer in [21], (see also [17]).

</details>


### [5] [Chebyshev polynomials on a Jordan arc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13445)
*Benedikt Buchecker,Benjamin Eichinger,Olof Rubin,Aron Wennman*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文给出了解析Jordan弧上Chebyshev多项式的渐近行为，证实了Christiansen-Simon-Zinchenko关于Widom 1969年预测的猜想，并得到了Chebyshev多项式自身的Szegő-Widom渐近公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决Chebyshev多项式在复平面解析Jordan弧上的渐近行为这一长期悬而未决的问题。这一猜想源于Christiansen-Simon-Zinchenko，其理论基础可追溯到Widom在1969年的预测。解决该问题对于理解复平面中一般紧集上多项式极值问题的渐近理论具有重要意义。

Method: 证明方法结合了多种分析工具：加权的Faber多项式用于处理解析弧上的多项式逼近结构；极值签名（extremal signatures）用于刻画极值多项式的符号模式；离散正交多项式提供了局部正交结构；Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund采样不等式用于控制采样点的误差。这些工具的有机结合使得对Chebyshev多项式的精确渐近分析成为可能。

Result: 成功地证实了Christiansen-Simon-Zinchenko提出的猜想，给出了解析Jordan弧上Chebyshev多项式的完整渐近展开，并导出了Chebyshev多项式本身的Szegő-Widom型渐近公式。

Conclusion: 该工作为复平面解析弧上极值多项式的渐近理论提供了完整的系统性解答，将Widom的经典预测推广到了当前框架，并为后续在更一般紧集上的推广研究奠定了方法论基础。

Abstract: We describe the asymptotics of Chebyshev polynomials on an analytic Jordan arc in the plane. This gives an affirmative answer to a conjecture of Christiansen-Simon-Zinchenko, based on predictions of Widom from 1969. The proof combines weighted Faber polynomials with extremal signatures, discrete orthogonal polynomials and a Marcinkiewicz-Zygmund sampling inequality, and yields Szegő-Widom asymptotics for the Chebyshev polynomials themselves.

</details>


### [6] [Fourier-invariant functions with dense zero sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13468)
*Andriy Bondarenko,Kristian Seip*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文对每个0≤β≤1/2构造了一个非零实值连续函数f_β∈L^1(R)∩L^2(R)，满足其Fourier变换等于自身（即Fourier不变），且在点列√n/[log(e+n)]^β上全部为零。β=0的情形否定了Radchenko和Viazovska关于其Fourier插值公式的一个问题。构造方法基于Fourier不变Hermite函数生成的再生核Hilbert空间尺度，利用Mehler公式识别再生核，并通过核估计证明该点列（加一个辅助点）是某个Hilbert空间的万有插值序列；但当β>1/2时结论不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Radchenko和Viazovska在Fourier插值公式研究中提出的一个开放问题：是否存在具有特定零点结构的非平凡Fourier不变函数。该问题关乎Fourier分析中插值理论的基本极限。

Method: 构造一族由Fourier不变的Hermite函数生成的再生核Hilbert空间（RKHS）。利用Mehler公式显式地识别这些空间的再生核，然后对这些核函数进行细致估计，证明在此框架下该点列（附加上一个辅助点）构成某个空间的万有插值序列，从而构造出所需的Fourier不变函数。

Result: 对每个0≤β≤1/2成功地构造了非零Fourier不变连续函数f_β∈L^1(R)∩L^2(R)，其在指定点列处取零。β=0的情形否定了Radchenko-Viazovska问题。同时证明了当β>1/2时该构造方法（及相应的插值性质）失败，表明1/2是临界指数。

Conclusion: 给出了Radchenko-Viazovska问题的一个否定的解答，并通过再生核Hilbert空间方法建立了Fourier不变函数存在性的精确临界阈值β=1/2，揭示了插值序列稀疏程度与Fourier不变函数存在性之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: For every $0\leqβ\leq1/2$, we construct a nonzero real-valued continuous function $f_β$ in $L^1(\mathbb R)\cap L^2(\mathbb R)$ such that $\widehat {f}_β=f_β$ and $f_β(\sqrt{n}/[\log(e+n)]^β)=0$ for all $n\geq 0$. The case $β=0$ settles in the negative a question raised by Radchenko and Viazovska regarding their Fourier interpolation formula. The construction uses a scale of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces generated by the Fourier-invariant Hermite functions. Applying the Mehler formula, we identify the reproducing kernels of these spaces. By suitable estimates of these kernels, we show that $(\sqrt{n}/[\log(e+n)]^β)$, with one auxiliary point added to it, is a universal interpolating sequence for at least one of the Hilbert spaces under consideration. However, this result fails when $β>1/2$.

</details>

### [7] [Rigidity theorems for cone structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12907)
*Tymon Frelik,Wojciech Kryński*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究可微流形上的锥结构（cone structures），特别是来自常微分方程并通过典范构造得到的各向同性锥结构（isotrivial cone structures）。证明以n维射影空间中一般曲线或三维射影空间中直纹面为模型的各向同性锥结构是平坦的，并讨论了在四维因果关系几何和无色散Lax系统中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锥结构是微分几何中重要的几何对象，各向同性锥结构（在每点射影化锥与固定子流形射影等价）的分类与平坦性问题尚未完全解决。本文聚焦于从常微分方程典范构造而来的一类锥结构，探究其在何种模型下必然平坦，并希望通过该结果加深对因果几何和Lax系统的理解。

Method: 利用典范构造将常微分方程与切丛中的锥结构联系起来，通过射影等价性定义各向同性；对以n维射影空间中一般曲线和三维射影空间中直纹面为模型的锥结构，证明其必须为平坦锥结构，并结合四维因果几何与无色散Lax系统进行分析。

Result: 证明了以n维射影空间中一般曲线或以三维射影空间中直纹面为模型的各向同性锥结构均为平坦的。

Conclusion: 各向同性锥结构在一般的曲线模型和直纹面模型下具有强刚性（平坦性），该结果对四维因果几何和无色散Lax系统具有重要的应用意义。

Abstract: Cone structures on differentiable manifolds are fields of cones in the tangent bundle. A cone structure is isotrivial, modeled on a fixed immersed submanifold of the projective space, if at each point the projectivised cone is projectively equivalent to that submanifold. We consider cone structures arising from ordinary differential equations via a canonical construction. We prove that isotrivial cone structures in this class, modeled on generic curves in the $n$-dimensional projective space or ruled surfaces in the three-dimensional projective space, are flat. We also discuss applications to causal geometries in four dimensions and dispersionless Lax systems.

</details>


### [8] [Rigidity of stable spacelike capillary hypersurfaces in de Sitter and Minkowski spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13115)
*Hui Ma,Jiaxu Ma,Mingxuan Yang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了de Sitter和Minkowski空间中紧致类光阴形毛细管超曲面的刚性定理：当支撑面为具有非负内曲率的类光全脐超曲面时，体积保持稳定性迫使超曲面全脐。利用光锥模型构造与支撑面相切的共形Killing场，推导出统一的对偶型Minkowski公式，并构造典型有限维测试函数族来检测脐性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究毛细管超曲面在Lorentzian空间形式中的体积保持稳定性与全脐性的关系，这是几何分析中刚性问题的核心，尤其涉及de Sitter和Minkowski空间中带支撑边界条件的超曲面稳定性分类问题。

Method: 采用光锥模型构造与支撑面相切的共形Killing向量场，推导出适用于所有Lorentzian空间形式的统一Minkowski型公式。得到的零均值函数满足非齐次Jacobi方程和线性化毛细管Robin边界条件，构成典型有限维测试函数族。通过有限迹恒等式（de Sitter情形补充非正Dirichlet Green修正项）检测脐性缺陷n|h|^2-H^2的定号性。

Result: 证明了对非负内曲率的类光全脐支撑面，任何非全脐超曲面都容许一个具有正二阶变分的容许测试函数，从而体积保持稳定必然迫使全脐性。对于负内曲率的支撑面，构造仍然成立，但存在唯一的类时参数方向使得有限迹不具定号性。

Conclusion: 在de Sitter和Minkowski空间中建立了毛细管超曲面的刚性定理，其稳定性临界情形完全由全脐超曲面刻画。该结果推广了经典的Alexandrov型和Bernstein型刚性结果至带边界的Lorentzian几何情形，并揭示了负曲率支撑面情形下刚性失效的本质原因。

Abstract: We prove a rigidity theorem for compact spacelike capillary hypersurfaces in de~Sitter and Minkowski spaces: volume-preserving stability forces total umbilicity when the support is a spacelike totally umbilical hypersurface of nonnegative intrinsic curvature. A finite-trace identity, supplemented in the de~Sitter cases by a nonpositive Dirichlet Green correction, detects the umbilicity defect \(n|h|^2-H^2\) with a definite sign; hence, every non-totally-umbilical hypersurface admits an admissible test function with positive second variation. Writing $Ξ_{k,m}$ for the quotient of $Ξ_k$ by translation through $m \geq 1$ fundamental periods, we study the Morse index and nullity of the subfamilies $Ξ_{k,2}$ and $Ξ_{3,m}$. In the $m=2$ case we prove for all $k \geq 3$ that $Ξ_{k,2}$ has Morse index $4k-3$ and nullity $3$. In the $k=3$ case we prove that there exists a real number $α^* \in (1/3,1/2)$ such that for all $m \geq 1$ the Morse index of $Ξ_{3,m}$ is $6m- 4 \lfloor mα^* \rfloor - 3$ and its nullity is $3$ unless $mα^*$ is an integer, in which case its nullity is $7$.

Abstract: We develop a topological framework for wave scattering by a medium with self-similar or prefractal geometry. Physical scaling identities motivate an auxiliary boundary-integral model that is periodic in logarithmic scale, and the Bloch-Floquet-Zak transform fiberizes its interscale coupling. For sufficiently small, well-separated components, equilibrium densities define a computable finite-dimensional projected matrix, while Riesz projections select the corresponding invariant spectral subspace of the full boundary symbol. Under the stated spectral-isolation and point-gap conditions, we prove that the determinant winding of the exact Riesz-reduced family agrees with that of the projected matrix. For the regular-simplex configurations, we obtain nonzero winding formulas and track the resulting local winding data across finite prefractal levels and along a geometric sequence of wavenumbers. We also obtain conditional results for different dilation centers and show that the Zak phase of the chiral Hermitianization is equal to $π$ times the point-gap winding modulo $2π$. Thus, the scale-periodic boundary model admits a rigorous topological reduction.

Method: 作者记录了若干基本关系：首先证明了对于任意可测基本域K和任意非零对偶向量ξ，方向方差满足⟨Cov_K ξ, ξ⟩ ≥ 1/12，这是尖锐的不等式。由此通过协方差算子的算子范数给出谱隙的下界λ_SG ≥ π²/(3‖Cov_K‖_op)。类似地，利用Hadwiger的经典论证，对等周轮廓和Cheeger常数建立了一致的尖锐结果。在Voronoi胞各向同性时，结合Slicing Problem的解得到普适下界。最后，作者引入截面镶嵌假设（sectional tiling hypothesis），在此条件下证明谱隙上界与协方差下界是等价的（差数值常数）。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 方向方差不等式⟨Cov_K ξ, ξ⟩ ≥ 1/12是尖锐的；(2) 由此导出的谱隙下界 λ_SG ≥ π²/(3‖Cov_K‖_op)；(3) 对于Cheeger常数有类似的Hadwiger论证的结果；(4) 当det Λ=1且Voronoi胞各向同性时，谱隙、Cheeger常数和最短对偶向量长度均普适地有正下界c>0，此即KLS猜想对所有平坦环面的正面回答；(5) 在截面镶嵌假设下，该谱隙界的逆不等式（直到常数）也成立。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了平坦环面谱参数与基本域协方差结构之间的精确联系，将KLS猜想在平坦环面情形归结为Voronoi胞的各向同性加上Slicing Problem的结论。虽然一般情形下无法得到维数无关的反向不等式，但在截面镶嵌条件下，谱隙界与协方差界之间是等价的。这为理解平坦环面（以及更一般的流形）上谱隙与几何参数的关系提供了新的视角。

Abstract: We record several elementary relations between spectral and isoperimetric parameters of a flat torus $\mathbb{T}_Λ= \mathbb{R}^n/Λ$ and the covariance structure of a fundamental domain $K$ for the lattice $Λ\subset \mathbb{R}^n$. For every measurable fundamental domain $K$ and nonzero vector $ξ$ in the dual lattice $Λ^*$, we observe the sharp directional variance estimate \[ \left\langle \operatorname{Cov}_K ξ,ξ\right\rangle \geq \frac{1}{12}. \] This yields a lower bound on the torus spectral gap $λ_{\mathrm{SG}}(\mathbb{T}_Λ)$ (equivalently, the length of the shortest nonzero dual vector $λ_1(Λ^*)$) in terms of the maximal covariance of $K$: \[ λ_{\mathrm{SG}}(\mathbb{T}_Λ) = 4π^2 λ_1(Λ^*)^2 \geq \frac{π^2}{3\left\|\operatorname{Cov}_K\right\|_{\mathrm{op}}}. \] Analogous sharp results are obtained for the isoperimetric profile and the Cheeger constant $D_{\mathrm{Che}}(\mathbb{T}_Λ)$ using an old argument of Hadwiger.
  In particular, when the Voronoi cell $K_Λ$ of a lattice with $\det Λ= 1$ is isotropic, the recent resolution of the Slicing Problem by Klartag and Lehec implies that \[ D_{\mathrm{Che}}(\mathbb{T}_Λ),\quad λ_{\mathrm{SG}}(\mathbb{T}_Λ),\quad λ_1(Λ^*) \geq c > 0, \] where $c > 0$ is a universal constant independent of dimension $n$; this may be thought of as a positive resolution of the Kannan--Lovász--Simonovits conjecture for all flat tori.
  While there are lattices $Λ$ and corresponding Voronoi cells $K = K_Λ$ for which no dimension-independent converse inequality to the spectral-gap bound above can hold, we show that under a certain sectional tiling hypothesis, this inequality is in fact an equivalence (up to numerical constants).

</details>

This leads to a minimax problem for finite configurations on $\mathbb{P}^1(\mathbb{Q})$, in which one seeks to minimize the maximum mesh ratio over all two-dimensional coordinate projections.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bombieri、Friedlander和Iwaniec在1986年证明了素数在模数至多x^{4/7-ε}的算术级数中均匀分布，该结果使用良好因子权重。本文旨在将此经典结果推广到卷积形式，并利用更精细的工具（Pascadi的三重良好因子卷积估计和不完全Kloosterman和估计）来改进已有的数论计数结果，特别是Wang的结果以及Ding和Wang关于P^+(p-1)分布的估计。

Abstract: In 1986, Bombieri, Friedlander and Iwaniec famously obtained that primes are equidistributed in arithmetic progressions to moduli up to $x^{4/7-\varepsilon}$, using well-factorable weights. In this paper, we apply Pascadi's triply-well-factorable convolution estimate and his estimation of incomplete Kloosterman sums to generalize this result to a convolutionform, which improves Wang's result under certain conditions. As for application, we consider the asymptotic density of $\#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}$ and $\#\{p\leq x:P^+(p-1)\geq p^c\}$, where $P^+(n)$ denote the largest prime factor of $n$.   \#\{n\leq x:P^+(n)<P^+(n+1)\}>0.296x

Abstract: Convolution identities and recursive formulas have long played a role in the theory of holomorphic modular forms and their applications. These have served both as striking formulas and as fundamentally useful tools for applications to combinatorics. Recently, there has been renewed interest in examples arising from non-holomorphic modular forms. These include the famous Hurwitz-Kronecker class number relations dating to 1885, and groundbreaking work of Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter from 2014. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter developed a theory of holomorphic projection for products of (vector-valued) harmonic Maass forms and holomorphic modular forms to produce many such formulas. This was related shortly thereafter by Duncan, Griffin, and Ono to replicable-type functions in the sense of Conway and Norton, and such recursions played a key role in their proof of the Umbral Moonshine Conjecture.
  Here, we develop new results on holomorphic projections of such functions which is more convenient for many natural cases. Imamoğlu, Raum, and Richter's choice corresponds to the case in which the generalized Pell equation $m^2 - Dn^2 = N$ has a square value for $D$, which has only finitely many solutions for a given value of $N$. Our main results cover the cases of general $D$, in particular those for which the Pell equation has infinitely many solutions.
  As an application, we resolve a recent conjecture of the second author. More generally, our approach yields 18 convolution identities for mock theta functions, which we boil down to 5 identities that can directly be used to prove the others. In the appendices, we also show how these identities can be proven by more direct $q$-series methods; however, the key utility of the holomorphic projection formulas is that they give a tool to automatically discover and verify such formulas.

Abstract: Let p > 3 be a prime, and let S_p denote the geometric isomorphism classes of supersingular elliptic curves in characteristic p whose j-invariants lie in F_p. We prove that the vectors (m_i(D) mod 2)_{i in S_p} span F_2^{S_p}. The key step reduces the parity of the representation numbers of Gross's ternary lattices to representation by rank-two sublattices perpendicular to Frobenius. Using Ibukiyama's explicit maximal orders, the resulting primitive binary forms are identified with those occurring in the Xiao--Zhou--Deng--Qu parametrization of supersingular elliptic curves over F_p. Class field theory and Chebotarev's theorem then allow the individual supersingular coordinates to be isolated.

Combining this parity theorem with formulas of Mestre and Gross--Kudla, we also prove that the modular degree of every positive-rank elliptic curve of prime conductor and root number +1 is divisible by 4. Consequently, Watkins' conjecture holds for all such curves of rank 2.

Abstract: We classify universal divisor-profile separation for coprime polynomial pairs of arbitrary degree and for all pairs of degree at most two. Abstract: In this paper, we extend the classical problem of studying the distribution of $k$-free integers in arithmetic progressions to the setting of arbitrary number fields. Using the language of ray class groups, we establish asymptotic formulas, together with error terms, for the number of $k$-free ideals of bounded norm lying in a given ray class. We also obtain improved error estimates in the cases of ideal class groups and narrow class groups by using sharper ideal counting asymptotics due to Landau. Finally, we show that for a $\mathbfΘ_n$-contraction $\mathbf{T}=(T_1,\dots,T_n)$ such that $T_n^*$ is a pure contraction, there exists an algebraic variety in $\mathbfΘ_n$ for which the von Neumann inequality holds on the intersection of the closure of the variety with the distinguished boundary of $\mathbfΘ_n$.

Abstract: We prove that every Kreiss bounded $C_0$-semigroup $(T_t)_{t\geq0}$ on a Hilbert space satisfies \[ \|T_t\|\leq C(1+t)^{1-\varepsilon_K}, \qquad t\geq0, \] where $\varepsilon_K>0$ depends explicitly only on the Kreiss constant. This improves the previously known estimate $O(t/\sqrt{\log(t+1)})$ and shows that every Kreiss bounded $C_0$-semigroup has a genuine polynomial gap below linear growth. Conclusion: 该工作不仅深化了对HRT猜想反例机制的理解，还将反例函数的衰减速度提升到了理论上最优的水平，表明在衰减速度意义下HRT反例的构造已经趋于完备。

Abstract: We build on the recent breakthrough of Faulhuber, Petersen, van Velthoven, and Voigtlaender that disproved the HRT conjecture with a Schwartz function and a $12$-point configuration. We give a human-readable treatment of their mechanism and find HRT counterexample functions with exponential (or faster) decay. By a result of Bownik and Speegle, this is the fastest possible decay for an HRT counterexample, up to a logarithmic factor in the exponent.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Jaguzović和Vujadinović提出的猜想：在多调和Fock空间中，Toeplitz算子的Schatten $p$-类隶属问题，特别地覆盖了$0<p<1$的范围（在$n\geq2$时此前未解决）。同时希望建立多调和情形与已知的全纯情形之间的精确联系，并获得范数比较的最优常数。

Method: 利用全纯与反全纯的正交分解（splitting）：多调和Fock空间分解为全纯部分、反全纯部分和对角/混合块。核心技巧包括：(1) 用正性来控制混合块被对角块的Schatten范数主导；(2) 构造正块算子的平方根分解以获得奇异值估计；(3) 通过单位球$B(z,r)$上的测度增长条件作为统一的刻画工具，建立与已知全纯Toeplitz算子结果的等价性。

Result: 对于所有$0<p<\infty$，证明了$T_\mu^{\mathrm{ph}}\in S_p \iff z\mapsto\mu(B(z,r))\in L^p(\mathbb{C}^n) \iff T_\mu\in S_p$。获得了Schatten范数的双边不等式$\|T_\mu\|_{S_p}^p\leq\|T_\mu^{\mathrm{ph}}\|_{S_p}^p\leq 2^{\max\{1,p\}}\|T_\mu\|_{S_p}^p$，并证明两个常数均为最优。该结果推广到任意对称赋范理想，并给出了精确的迹恒等式。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了多调和Fock空间上Toeplitz算子的Schatten $p$-类刻画问题，对于$n\geq2$验证了Jaguzović和Vujadinović的猜想，并将结果扩展到$0<p<1$的全范围。证明了多调和算子的Schatten范数被全纯情形（通过常数$2^{\max\{1,p\}}$）精确控制，且常数不可改进。方法论上，全纯/反全纯分解中的正性控制和平方根分解为多调和分析中的算子理想刻画提供了新工具。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a positive Borel measure on $\C^n$. For every $0<p<\infty$, we prove that the Toeplitz operator $T_μ^{\mathrm{ph}}$ induced by $μ$ on pluriharmonic Fock space belongs to $\Sp_p$ if and only if $z\mapstoμ(B(z,r))$ belongs to $L^p(\C^n)$ for one, or equivalently every, $r>0$; this is also equivalent to Schatten membership of the corresponding holomorphic Toeplitz operator $T_μ$. For $n\geq2$, this settles a conjecture of Jaguzović and Vujadinović, and the result includes the range $0<p<1$ in every dimension. Moreover, \[
  \norm{T_μ}_{\Sp_p}^p
  \leq\norm{T_μ^{\mathrm{ph}}}_{\Sp_p}^p
  \leq2^{\max\{1,p\}}\norm{T_μ}_{\Sp_p}^p, \] and both constants are optimal. More generally, we obtain a sharp comparison for every symmetrically normed ideal. The proof uses the holomorphic and antiholomorphic splitting: positivity controls the mixed block by the diagonal blocks, while a square root factorization of the positive block operator yields the singular value estimates. We also obtain an exact trace identity.

</details>

Abstract: It is well-established that single-axis Transverse Ray Transform tomography data from high-energy X-ray diffraction is insufficient for general reconstruction of three-dimensional elastic strain. In this paper we show that, when combined with the constraint of mechanical equilibrium, this problem becomes uniquely solvable for isotropic elastic samples with known elastic constants and non-zero Poisson's ratio. Building on the reconstruction framework of Desai and Lionheart [N.M. Desai, W.R.B. Lionheart, An explicit reconstruction algorithm for the transverse ray transform of a second rank tensor field from three axis data, Inverse Problems 32 (11) (2016) 115009], we formulate this constrained single-axis tomography problem in the Fourier domain and show that the resulting system is invertible everywhere outside a finite collection of measure zero characteristic planes. In the case of bounded samples, this is sufficient to establish uniqueness for reconstructions. We further derive conditional stability estimates that quantify the regularity requirements associated with reconstruction of the various strain components.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the initial value problem of the sixth-order Boussinesq equation with quadratic and cubic nonlinearities in arbitrary spatial dimensions. Abstract: We prove uniqueness in the Lorentzian Calderón problem in a semiglobal setting, comparing a potentially large perturbation of the Minkowski metric against a small one. We then establish the existence of Delaunay-type periodic singular solutions. Abstract: We generalize the classical vector-valued tangential and normal trace theory on Lipschitz domains to the setting of non-Lipschitz $H^1$-extension domains, which includes domains with fractal boundaries such as the Koch snowflake. We define and study these operators based on the surjectivity of the trace operator for elements of $H^1(Ω)$ and the Hilbert structure of the associated trace space. The normal trace operator is defined on $\Hdiv$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$. A generalized Stokes formula allows us to introduce the tangential trace operator on $\Hcurl$ and $\Hc^1(Ω)$ in two and three dimensions. Following the approach of Buffa, Costabel and Sheen (2002) for Lipschitz domains, we define two abstract tangential boundary spaces as images of these trace operators, establish their Hilbert structure, and construct an abstract rotation operator linking them, in place of the geometric rotation based on the normal vector. We also extend Costabel's (1991) coercive bilinear form approach for Maxwell's equations to non-Lipschitz domains, yielding a theory that supports the treatment of the Hodge-Dirac operator and Green's formulas and enables the solution of boundary-value problems for the $\rotv \rotv +1$ operator within the $H^1$-extension domains framework.

Abstract: We establish exact anisotropic $L^p$-Hardy and Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg identities associated with the Minkowski functional $\|\cdot\|_K$ and the anisotropic radial derivative $\mathcal R_K$, where $1<p<\infty$ and $K\subset\RN$ is a smooth and strictly convex body containing the origin in its interior, not necessarily origin-symmetric. These identities contain explicit nonnegative $R_p$-remainders and yield sharp anisotropic radial $L^p$-Hardy and $L^p$-Caffarelli-Kohn-Nirenberg inequalities. In the noncritical parameter regions, we characterize the full family of extremal functions in the natural weighted completion space, while in the critical case the sharp constant is not attained by any nonzero function in that space. As applications, we establish sharp anisotropic $L^p$-Heisenberg uncertainty principles and obtain explicit anisotropic Gaussian-type optimizers. Finally, letting $K^*$ denote the polar body of $K$, we derive norm-based anisotropic gradient inequalities associated with the origin-symmetric body $K^*\cap(-K^*)$. When $K$ is origin-symmetric, the corresponding gradient constants are sharp, including those in the critical case.

Abstract: In the present paper, we consider the Cauchy problem of a system of three nonlinear Schrödinger equations with quadratic nonlinearity. The second is an improvement of global well-posedness for oscillating initial data. We also prove the orbital stability of the ground state sets with small speed parameter.

Abstract: We study the multiscale behavior of a coupled visco-poroelastic system arising in the modelling of fibre-reinforced hydrogels (FIHs) used in tissue engineering scaffolds. The two phases are coupled through continuity of displacement and traction across their shared interface. Both the fibre scaffold and the hydrogel phase are connected, so that mechanical forces can be transmitted through the composite and interstitial fluid can flow directly through the hydrogel network.
  Starting from a microscopic ($\varepsilon$-scale) model, we derive uniform a-priori estimates and establish well-posedness via a Rothe time-discretisation argument for both the case of standard Biot fluid content $η=0$ and the case of viscous fluid content $η=αδ>0$. We then perform a rigorous two-scale homogenization in the limit $\varepsilon \to 0$ using periodic unfolding. In addition to the usual effective elasticity, storage, coupling, and permeability coefficients, the homogenized constitutive laws contain nonlocal-in-time memory terms generated by the microscopic viscoelastic relaxation. All effective coefficients and memory kernels are explicitly characterized in terms of the microscale geometry and material parameters.

Method: 对arXiv源码和MATLAB归档进行版本固定的源码级审计，检查实现中的数值边界、重构步骤等关键环节；使用精确算术构造可再现的反例来验证发现的缺陷。

Conclusion: 审计发现不否认原论文所声称的定理本身或其解析约化过程的正确性，但证明发布的计算证书无法支撑其声称的结论，需要后续修正工作提供一个结构更简单、机器辅助量更少的正确证明。

Abstract: We audit the exact-arithmetic certificate in arXiv:2310.19781v2, its 2025 \emph{Communications in Mathematical Physics} version, and their shared MATLAB archive. CMP corrects none of the audited items; we therefore treat both versions together and use the openly accessible arXiv source for exact provenance. We found 11 proof-affecting defects (alongside typographical slips) and give reproducible exact counterexamples to several implemented bounds and reconstruction steps. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first independent, version-pinned, source-level audit of a published computer-assisted proof (CAP) in analysis to identify multiple proof-affecting defects in its released computational certificate. The findings do not refute the intended theorem or its analytic reduction, but they show that the published certificate does not prove the claimed conclusion. Future work will give a corrected, structurally simpler proof with substantially less machine assistance.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者通过构造一族光滑对称 3×3 系数矩阵 A_m，使得椭圆常数固定（上下界分别为 1 和 2^81），但对应的解 u_m 满足共同的 Dirichlet 数据和一致 L^∞ 有界性，同时其梯度的 L^1 范数在 B_1 上无界。通过这一族具体构造的解序列来证明不存在内 W^{1,1} 估计。

Result: 证明了在三维空间中，对于非散度形式的均匀椭圆方程，不存在仅依赖椭圆性的内 W^{1,1} 估计；从而也不存在任何 p≥1 的 W^{1,p} 估计。同时，构造的极限过程给出了一个 L^1 极限的系数矩阵，其对应的一致收敛解极限不在 BV_loc(B_1) 中。

Conclusion: 本文通过在三维空间中构造具体的反例，否定了 Nadirashvili、Tkachev 和 Vlăduţ 提出的关于内 W^{1,1} 估计的存在性问题。这一结果表明，在三维情况下，仅依赖椭圆常数的梯度可积性估计是不存在的，即使对于光滑系数矩阵的极限（可测系数）也是如此。

Abstract: For each nonnegative integer $m$, we construct smooth symmetric $3\times 3$ coefficient matrices $A_m$ satisfying the fixed ellipticity bound \[
  I\leq A_m\leq 2^{81}I \] for which the smooth solutions of uniformly elliptic equations in nondivergence form \[
  \text{tr}(A_m(x)D^2 u_m)=A_m(x):D^2u_m=0\qquad\text{in }B_2\subset {\mathbb R}^3 \] have common Dirichlet data, satisfy $\|u_m\|_{L^\infty(B_2)}\leq1$, but \[
  \lim_{m\to \infty}\|Du_m\|_{L^1(B_1)}=\infty. \] Thus, there is no interior $W^{1,1}$ estimate depending only on ellipticity in dimension three, and consequently no such $W^{1,p}$ estimate for any $p\geq1$. This resolves in the negative an open question raised by Nadirashvili, Tkachev, and Vlăduţ. The construction also gives a uniformly convergent limit $u\notin \text{BV}_{\rm loc}(B_1)$ for a measurable uniformly elliptic coefficient matrix obtained as an $L^1$ limit of the $A_m$.

</details>


### [83] [Memory Stabilizes Spontaneous Particle Aggregation: A Linearized Vlasov--Fokker--Planck Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13440)
*Jan Haskovec*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文对带记忆的随机自发聚集模型的平均场方程——Vlasov-Fokker-Planck方程进行了线性化稳定性分析。当所有弛豫速率相等时，证明了无记忆模型中不稳定的每个傅里叶模态都存在唯一的临界弛豫参数；对于径向对称的距离递减核，记忆不会使原本稳定的均匀平衡态失稳，但可能改变不稳定模态集合。最后利用Crandall-Rabinowitz定理得到从均匀态分岔的空间非均匀稳态解分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解记忆效应在自发聚集模型中的作用机制，特别是记忆如何影响系统的稳定性特征。此前已有粒子模拟观察到长时间记忆导致的粗化（coarsening）效应，但缺乏系统的理论分析，本文旨在从线性化稳定性角度给出严格的理论解释。

Method: (1) 用K个内部变量构成的链来建模记忆，建立Vlasov-Fokker-Planck方程；(2) 刻画空间均匀平衡态并推导标量色散关系；(3) 在均匀弛豫速率的假设下，对每个傅里叶模态分析记忆与稳定性之间的定量关系；(4) 对径向对称且距离递减的核证明记忆不能引发整体失稳；(5) 用归一化顶帽（top-hat）核做数值验证；(6) 在非退化与正则性假设下应用Crandall-Rabinowitz定理推导分岔解。

Result: (1) 均匀弛豫速率下，每个无记忆模型中不稳定的傅里叶模态都有唯一的临界弛豫参数；(2) 记忆可能使与核的负傅里叶系数相关的个别模态失稳，但对径向对称距离递减核，记忆不能使无记忆模型中稳定均匀平衡态变得不稳定；(3) 数值实验表明增加有效记忆长度逐步稳定各傅里叶模态，高频先于低频被稳定，与粒子模拟的粗化现象一致；(4) 在适当假设下得到空间非均匀稳态解的分岔分支。

Conclusion: 记忆的引入不能创造新的总体不稳定性（对于径向对称递减核），但可以重塑不稳定模态的分布，表现为逐步稳定模态并优先稳定高频，从而解释并支持了粒子模拟中观察到的粗化效应。分岔分析进一步揭示了从均匀态到空间非均匀结构的演化路径。

Abstract: We perform a linearized stability analysis of the Vlasov--Fokker--Planck equation obtained as the mean-field description of a stochastic spontaneous aggregation model with memory. Memory is represented by a chain of $K$ internal variables. We characterize the spatially homogeneous equilibria and derive a scalar dispersion relation for spatially inhomogeneous perturbations. When all relaxation rates are equal, we show that every Fourier mode that is unstable in the memoryless model possesses a unique critical relaxation parameter: sufficiently long memory stabilizes the mode, whereas it remains unstable for short memory. Although memory may destabilize individual modes associated with negative Fourier coefficients of the sensing kernel, we prove that, for radially symmetric distance-decreasing kernels, it cannot destabilize a homogeneous equilibrium that is stable in the memoryless model. Thus, memory cannot create an overall instability of an otherwise stable homogeneous state, although it may change the set of the unstable modes. We present a numerical example for the normalized top-hat kernel, demonstrating that increasing the effective memory length successively stabilizes the Fourier modes, with higher frequencies being stabilized before lower ones. This is consistent with the coarsening effect observed in recent particle simulations. Finally, under suitable nondegeneracy and regularity assumptions, we use the Crandall--Rabinowitz theorem to obtain branches of spatially inhomogeneous stationary solutions bifurcating from the homogeneous state.

</details>


### [84] [Fundamental Gaps for the Dirichlet \(p\)-Laplacian with Convex Potentials: Sharp One-Dimensional Bounds and a Higher-Dimensional Dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13443)
*Rui Chen,Daniel Hauer*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究了有界凸区域上带凸势的Dirichlet p-Laplace算子的基本间隙（fundamental gap）。作者通过正则化和两点极大值原理证明了正第一特征函数的log-凹性；在N≥2时，通过光滑凸区域的退化识别出p=2处的尖锐过渡：1<p<2时间隙消失，p=2时量级为D^{-2}，p>2时发散。对p≥2建立了退化加权Poincaré不等式，进而得到L^p-Poincaré不等式的定量稳定性估计和维度无关的基本间隙界；零势情形下进一步得到包含第一特征值和直径的增强间隙估计。对p>2证明了直径归一化间隙极小元的存在性及其在p↓2时的退化性。最后对N=1证明了涉及π_p的锐利不等式，等号仅在常数势时取得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基本间隙（即前两个特征值之差）是谱几何中的经典问题，它刻画了特征函数的能量分离程度。经典结果如Andreu-Van den Berg对线性Laplace算子（p=2）的间隙估计已被广泛研究，但对于非线性p-Laplace算子的基本间隙行为，尤其是对参数p的依赖关系，此前缺乏系统理解。本文旨在回答p-Laplace基本间隙如何随p变化这一核心问题，特别是p=2是否构成临界点，以及凸势条件下能否获得锐利且维度无关的估计。

Method: 本文采用多种分析工具：(1)通过正则化技巧和两点极大值原理（two-point maximum principle）证明第一正特征函数关于凸区域的log-凹性；(2)利用光滑凸区域的退化构造（collapsing smooth convex domains）来刻画p依赖的间隙行为，识别p=2处的尖锐相变；(3)对p≥2建立退化的加权Poincaré不等式，由此推导L^p-Poincaré不等式的定量稳定性估计及维度无关的间隙上界；(4)对p>2利用变分方法证明直径归一化间隙极小元的存在性；(5)对N=1维情形通过直接计算证明包含π_p的锐利不等式。

Result: 主要结果：(1)在凸区域上证明了p-Laplace正第一特征函数的log-凹性；(2)在N≥2时证明了p=2是基本间隙行为的临界点：1<p<2时间隙在区域退化时趋于零，p=2时保持D^{-2}量级，p>2时发散；(3)对p≥2建立了维度无关的基本间隙上界；(4)零势情形下得到了改进的间隙估计，同时涉及第一特征值和直径；(5)对p>2证明了归一化间隙极小元的存在性且随p↓2退化；(6)一维情形得到锐利下界 λ_{2,p}-λ_{1,p} ≥ (p-1)(2^p-1)(π_p/D)^p，等号仅在常数势时成立。

Conclusion: 本文系统刻画了凸区域上带凸势的Dirichlet p-Laplace基本间隙对参数p的依赖，确立了p=2作为临界指数的地位，建立了凸势下维度无关的间隙估计，并在N=1情形给出了锐利不等式。这些结果深化了非线性椭圆算子的谱理论，为理解p-Laplace算子的谱学性质提供了重要的定量工具，同时提出的正则化方法和两点极值原理为后续研究p-Laplace及其他非线性算子提供了可推广的技术路线。

Abstract: We study fundamental gaps for the Dirichlet \(p\)-Laplacian on bounded convex domains with convex potentials. We prove log-concavity of the positive first eigenfunction by a regularization and two-point maximum principle. For \(N\geq2\), we identify a sharp transition at \(p=2\) through collapsing smooth convex domains: the gap vanishes for \(1<p<2\), remains of order \(D^{-2}\) for \(p=2\), and diverges for \(p>2\). For \(p\geq2\) and convex potentials, we first establish a degenerate weighted Poincaré inequality, which yields quantitative stability estimates for the \(L^p\)-Poincaré inequality and, in turn, dimension-free bounds for the fundamental gap; for zero potential, we further obtain an enhanced gap estimate involving both the first eigenvalue and the diameter. We also prove existence of diameter-normalized gap minimizers for \(p>2\) and show that they degenerate as \(p\downarrow2\). Finally, for $N=1,$ we prove the sharp inequality \[ λ_{2,p}(I_D,V)-λ_{1,p}(I_D,V) \geq (p-1)(2^p-1)\left(\frac{π_p}{D}\right)^p \] for every \(p>1\) and every convex potential, with equality precisely for constant potentials.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove that for every integer $N\geq 3$ and $α\geq \frac{1}{2}$, Beckner's inequality \[ \fracα{2}\int_{\mathbb{S}^N}u(P_{N}u) dw+(N-1)!\int_{\mathbb{S}^N}u dw-\frac{(N-1)!}{N}\log\int_{\mathbb{S}^N}e^{Nu} dw\geq 0 \] holds for every $u\in H^{\frac{N}{2}}(\mathbb{S}^N)$ whose center of mass is at the origin. The proof is mainly based on an integral representation formula and a rigidity theorem for stable critical points. Hence, we answer the generalized Chang-Yang conjecture positively for every integer $N\geq 3$.

</details>

Abstract: We define two notions of Morita equivalence for graded C*-algebras (graded Morita equivalence and homogeneous Morita equivalence) and provide Brown-Green-Rieffel Stabilization Type Theorems for both notions of graded equivalence. Lastly, we incorporate Cartan subalgebras with totally disconnected spectra and obtain Brown-Green-Rieffel Stabilization Type Theorems for these cases.

Abstract: We prove dynamical comparison for Deaconu--Renault groupoids associated to minimal surjective non-injective local homeomorphisms of compact metrizable spaces with finite Lebesgue covering dimension. As a corollary, the associated $C^*$-algebras are UCT Kirchberg algebras, recovering results by Carlsen--Thomsen via dynamical methods. In the zero-dimensional case, our result also verifies Matui's AH-conjecture for these groupoids using a recent breakthrough of Xin Li. Our proof combines techniques from both the purely infinite and stably finite regimes: We construct partial actions of non-abelian free groups as suitable ``large subgroupoids'' and establish comparison properties for these using the paradoxical towers technique developed by Gardella--Geffen--Kranz--Naryshkin. The boundary of the subgroupoid is controlled by a groupoid version of the topological small boundary property which we deduce from finite covering dimension of the unit space. As a byproduct, we prove the classical small boundary property for minimal actions of countable discrete groups on finite-dimensional compact metrizable spaces without any freeness assumption.

</details>

Abstract: The binary-tree height recursion defines an algebra $\mathcal{H}$ on $\mathbb{N}\cup\{-\infty\}$, with join given by $\max$ and product \[ a\star b=\max\{a,b\}+1. \] We show that weighted evaluation of a labelled tree depends only on the greatest depth of each label. Single-tree profiles are exactly the vectors satisfying the binary Kraft inequality, while finite joins realize every vector in $\bigl(\mathbb{N}\cup\{-\infty\}\bigr)^n$; hence the $n$-variable term operations form the free algebra $\mathcal{H}^n$. We also classify $\mathcal{H}$'s compatible semilattice operation, subalgebras, endomorphisms, congruences, and finite quotients, and recover the dyadic-composition spectrum at the full-linear boundary.

</details>

Result: 本文解决了Tuite和Erskine关于几乎混合Moore图总正则性的公开问题，并由此证明了直径至少为3的已知的三个几乎混合Moore图是仅有的此类混合图。

Abstract: The degree/diameter problem asks for the largest order of a graph with a given diameter and maximum vertex degree. This has been widely studied and given rise to a recent variation for mixed graphs (graphs with both undirected edges and directed arcs), where an additional bound is placed on the maximum directed out-degree of any vertex. Both problems have applications to network design. Counting the possible number of vertices at each distance from a given vertex gives a bound on the order of a mixed graph satisfying the degree and diameter constraints (the mixed Moore bound). In this paper, we settle an open problem posed by Tuite and Erskine concerning the total regularity of mixed graphs whose order is one less than the mixed Moore bound (almost mixed Moore graphs). We use this result to show that the three known almost mixed Moore graphs of diameter at least three are the only such mixed graphs.

</details>


### [90] [A Graph Theoretical Approach to Optimizing Minimum Italian Domination Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12449)
*Muhammad Zeeshan,Nahid Akhtar,Muhammad Faisal Nadeem*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对Toeplitz图族中的意大利支配数（Roman 2-支配数）进行了系统分析，给出了多个图族的意大利支配数的完整结果，并确定了支配数随生成元值增大而变化的具体临界值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 针对Toeplitz图族（由Toeplitz矩阵的常对角元构造），逐一分析不同生成元取值条件下的意大利支配数，通过图论证明与构造性方法推导支配数的精确值。

Result: 给出了多个Toeplitz图族意大利支配数的完整结果，并识别出支配数随生成元值增大而发生变化的具体临界值，展示了支配数的变化规律。

Conclusion: 本文为Toeplitz图的意大利支配数提供了全面且系统的刻画，明确了支配数随生成元变化的关键转折点，为该领域后续研究及网络安全等实际应用提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: A classical problem in graph theory known as the Italian domination number(also called Roman 2-domination number), involves assigning labels of 0, 1,or 2 to each node v. The goal is to ensure that every node with a label of 0 has a sum of labels of the nodes in its closed neighborhood that is 2 or greater. In computer systems, it is coined encompassing a robust cyber security strategy that will protect networks from potential threats, such as hacking, malware, and unauthorized access, by deploying security measures to provide the highest level of protection while reducing the misuse of resources. Toeplitz graphs are a special kind of graphs built over Toeplitz matrices from linear algebra, which are matrices with constant straight diagonal members. In this paper, we provide a detailed analysis regarding the Italian domination numbers for every Toeplitz graph family. We provide comprehensive results on Italian domination numbers across multiple graph families and identify the specific values at which the Italian domination number alters with increasing generator values.

</details>


### [91] [Online balancing of vectors with small coordinates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12490)
*Antonios Hmadi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究在线符号翻转（online signing）问题：给定预先固定并顺序揭示的向量序列 v_1,...,v_T ∈ B_2^m，其中每个向量的无穷范数不超过 d^{-1/2}，构造随机化在线符号选择，使得所有前缀和的无穷范数以高概率不超过一个绝对常数（前缀差异为 O(1)）。论文给出了失败概率上界 CT·exp(-cd/ln²(ed))，证明当 d ≥ C·ln(3T/ε)[ln(e+ln(3T/ε))]² 时，常数前缀差异以至少 1-ε 的概率成立。特别地，每个至多 d 个非零坐标的 [-1,1]^m 中向量序列，存在前缀差异为 O(√d) 的在线符号配置。论文还证明了非均匀版本（失败概率依赖于个体参数 d_t=‖v_t‖_∞^{-2}），下界表明当 d=o(ln T) 时通用常数前缀差异不可能，识别出紧致势方法的 ln²d 屏障，并将论证推广到具有二次光滑性估计的一般对称目标体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线符号翻转和前缀差异问题是组合学与在线算法中的经典问题，源于拼图/零和序列的在线配置研究。经典Spencer定理给出了离线情况下 O(√d) 的差异界，但在线场景（每次决策不可反悔）中，已有方法仅能达到对数级的界。本文的目标是确定在向量具有稀疏性/小范数约束时，在线符号翻转能否实现常数级的前缀差异，并刻画所需条件（即 d 的最小规模）以及失败概率的精确指数衰减速率。

Method: 本文采用紧致势方法（compact potential method）的变体。核心思想是构造一个定义在某个紧致集合上的势函数，通过随机化在线符号选择（Rademacher 变量）逐步推进势能下降，利用二次光滑性估计（quadratic smoothness estimate）来控制每一步的期望增量。论文识别出该方法存在 ln²d 的屏障（即失败概率中含 ln²(ed) 因子），并通过精确的势能分析和概率累加论证获得最终界。此外，作者还提供了非均匀版本（根据 d_t 动态调整）以及适配一般对称目标体的推广，并通过下界构造说明无法在 d=o(ln T) 时实现通用常数前缀差异。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 随机化在线符号算法使得前缀差异超过 6L 的概率 ≤ CT·exp(-cd/ln²(ed))；(2) 由此得出常数前缀差异在 d ≥ C·ln(3T/ε)[ln(e+ln(3T/ε))]² 时以 1-ε 概率成立；(3) 对每个至多 d 个非零坐标的向量序列，在线符号配置的前缀差异为 O(√d)，失败概率 ≤ CT·exp[-cd/ln²(ed)]；(4) 证明非均匀版本，失败概率依赖各时刻的 d_t=‖v_t‖_∞^{-2}；(5) 下界表明 d=o(ln T) 时通用常数前缀差异不可能。指出了紧致势方法中 ln²d 的固有屏障。

Conclusion: 本文在理论上基本刻画了在线稀疏向量序列的符号翻转问题：当稀疏度参数 d 足够大（对数级别乘以迭代对数平方）时，常数前缀差异在概率意义下可实现；失败概率呈 exp(-cd/ln²(ed)) 的超指数衰减。同时证明了 d=o(ln T) 时通用常数界的不可能性，因此结果在 d 的量级上近乎精确。论文还统一了方法论的视角——识别出 ln²d 屏障源于紧致势方法的本质限制，并将框架推广至一般的二次光滑对称凸体，为后续在线差异理论提供了通用工具。

Abstract: Let $v_1,\ldots,v_T\in B_2^m$ be fixed in advance and revealed sequentially, and assume that $\|v_t\|_\infty\leqslant d^{-1/2}$ for some $d\geqslant 1$ and every $1\leqslant t\leqslant T$. There are absolute constants $L,C,c>0$ and a randomized online signing such that $$\mathbb{P}\left\{\max_{k\leqslant T}\left\|\sum_{t=1}^k\varepsilon_t v_t\right\|_\infty>6L\right\} \leqslant CT\exp\left(-\frac{cd}{\ln^2(ed)}\right).$$ Consequently, constant prefix discrepancy holds with probability at least $1-\varepsilon$ once $d$ is at least $C\ln\frac{3T}{\varepsilon}\left[\ln\left(e+\ln\frac{3T}{\varepsilon}\right)\right]^2$. In particular, every fixed sequence of vectors $a_t\in[-1,1]^m$ with at most $d$ nonzero coordinates admits an online signing with prefix discrepancy $O(\sqrt d)$ and failure probability at most $CT\exp[-cd/\ln^2(ed)]$. We also prove a nonuniform version in which the failure probability depends on the individual parameters $d_t=\|v_t\|_\infty^{-2}$, and a lower bound showing that a universal constant prefix discrepancy is impossible when $d=o(\ln T)$. We identify the corresponding $\ln^2 d$ barrier for the compact-potential method and extend the argument to general symmetric target bodies admitting a quadratic smoothness estimate.

</details>


### [92] [On the minimum vertex cover of snarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12491)
*Gustavo Fernandes,Atílio G. Luiz*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究snark图（连通的、无桥的、四次边色数的三次图）的最小顶点覆盖问题。首先证明对于任意snark图，判断是否存在大小不超过k的顶点覆盖是NP完全问题；其次确定了若干snark图子类（包括Flower snarks、Goldberg snarks、Generalized Blanuša snarks和Loupekine snarks）的顶点覆盖数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顶点覆盖是图论中的经典NP完全问题，而snark图作为三次图中的特殊类别（具有四条边色数），在图论尤其是四色定理的研究中具有重要地位。然而对于snark图的顶点覆盖问题此前尚未有系统的研究，本文旨在填补这一空白，既从复杂性角度证明该问题的难度，又从具体图类角度给出精确的顶点覆盖数值。

Method: 本文采用两种研究方法：(1) 通过归约构造证明任意snark图判定是否存在大小≤k的顶点覆盖是NP完全问题；(2) 针对Flower snarks、Goldberg snarks、Generalized Blanuša snarks和Loupekine snarks等具体snark子类，利用其图结构特征推导并确定各自的顶点覆盖数τ(G)。

Result: 本文获得两方面的结果：一是证明了在任意snark图上判定顶点覆盖大小是否不超过给定正整数k是NP完全问题；二是精确确定了Flower snarks、Goldberg snarks、Generalized Blanuša snarks和Loupekine snarks这些snark子类的顶点覆盖数。

Conclusion: 本文系统性地研究了snark图的顶点覆盖问题，揭示了该问题在一般snark类上的计算困难性（NP完全），同时为若干重要的snark子类给出了顶点覆盖数的精确公式，为snark图的进一步研究提供了新的组合参数视角。

Abstract: A vertex cover of a graph $G$ is a subset of vertices $C \subseteq V(G)$ such that every edge of $G$ is incident to at least one vertex in $C$. The vertex cover number of $G$ is the minimum cardinality of a vertex cover of $G$ and is denoted by $τ(G)$. A snark is a connected, bridgeless, cubic graph that has an edge chromatic number of four, meaning its edges cannot be properly colored with only three colors. In this work, we investigate the problem of determining the value of a minimum vertex cover for classes of snark graphs. Given a positive integer $k$, we firstly prove that determining whether an arbitrary snark has a vertex cover $C$ with size $|C| \leq k$ is an NP-complete problem. Secondly, we determine the vertex cover number $τ(G)$ for several subclasses of snark graphs, such as Flower snarks, Goldberg snarks, Generalized Blanuša snarks and Loupekine snarks.

</details>


### [93] [The balanced upper chromatic number of linear hypergraphs and the $n$-cube over $t$ elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12516)
*Gabriela Araujo-Pardo,Silvia Fernández-Merchant,Adriana Hansberg,Dolores Lara,Amanda Montejano,Déborah Oliveros*
